- **bespunky-communication**: How Claude speaks: ships the 'Pitch to the listener' output style, every answer pitched to the person reading it. Brief by default, for a reader who stepped out; full depth the moment they engage. Default in BeSpunky projects; elsewhere pick it with /output-style. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/communication))
- **bespunky-design-system**: BeSpunky design-system skills - styling as a SYSTEM on any stack (Angular is one adapter). design-system-first: the discipline - every visual value is a token from the design system, no raw hex, magic px or style reach-ins, and a pattern's second occurrence is promoted into the DS. design-tokens-and-theming: the techniques - token tiers, CSS custom properties as the runtime layer, a zero-output SASS API, light/dark/brand modes, encapsulation, and the DS library's entry points. Plus resumable-state (a deliberate home for view and working state) and typed-reactive-navigation (typed per-domain navigation, never a raw router call), each with an Angular adapter.
